By the Burkholder-Gundy-Davis inequality, we have

\displaystyle C\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|\int_{0}^{t}(c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s),g_{\gamma_{2}}(c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s))dW_{2}(s))\right|^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}\left|g_{\gamma_{2}}(c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s))\right|^{2}_{L_{\text{HS}}(H_{1},L^{2})}ds\right)^{\frac{m^{*}}{2r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}\right|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{2}}+C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|g_{\gamma_{2}}(c_{\kappa}(s))\right|^{2}_{L_{\text{HS}}(H_{1},L^{2})}ds\right)^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}\right|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{2}}+C\mathbb{E}\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}ds.

We note that

\displaystyle\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\xi_{1}(s)-\xi_{2}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{-s^{**}}}^{2}ds\right)^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}+\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}ds\right)^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\xi_{1}(s)-\xi_{2}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{-s^{**}}}^{m^{*}}ds\right)^{\frac{2}{r^{*}}}+C\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{2}}ds

\displaystyle=C\left(\mathbb{E}\left|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\right|^{m^{*}}_{\mathbb{X}}\right)^{\frac{2}{r^{*}}}+C\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{2}}ds.

Taking the power
\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}
and the expectation in the inequality (
3.46
) and using these last estimate, we arrive at

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|{c_{\kappa}^{(1)}-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}}(s)\right|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{2}}+\alpha_{0}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|{c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}}(s)\right|^{2}_{H^{1}_{2}}ds\right)^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\mathbb{E}\left|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\right|^{m^{*}}_{\mathbb{X}}\right)^{\frac{2}{r^{*}}}+C(\kappa,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{{\bf H}}^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+C\mathbb{E}\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{2}}ds.

By the Gronwall lemma we obtain the assertion.

Next, we consider the difference
n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}
, i.e. part (iii).
Applying the Itô formula to the function
\phi(n):=|n|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}

and using integration by parts gives

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(t)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(t)|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}+\frac{r_{n}}{2^{q-1}}\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}ds

\displaystyle\leq 2\theta\int_{0}^{T}(\nabla^{-1}(\xi_{1}(s)-\xi_{2}(s)),\nabla^{-1}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)))ds

\displaystyle\quad-2\chi\int_{0}^{T}(\nabla^{-1}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)),\nabla^{-1}(\mbox{div}(\xi_{1}(s)\nabla c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\xi_{2}(s)\nabla c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)))ds

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{T}(\nabla^{-1}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)),\nabla^{-1}({\bf u}^{(1)}(s)\cdot\nabla\xi_{1}(s)-{\bf u}^{(2)}(s)\cdot\nabla\xi_{2}(s)))ds

\displaystyle\quad+2\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|\int_{0}^{t}\left(\nabla^{-1}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)),\nabla^{-1}g_{\gamma_{1}}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s))\,dW_{1}(s)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{T}\left|g_{\gamma_{1}}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s))\right|^{2}_{L_{\text{HS}}(H_{1},H_{2}^{-1})}ds

\displaystyle=I_{0}+I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}+I_{4}.

Next, we calculate
I_{0}
. Here, we use the embedding of
H^{-s^{**}}_{r^{*}}({{\mathcal{O}}})
into
H^{-1}_{2}({{\mathcal{O}}})
. we note that for any
\varepsilon>0
,

\displaystyle I_{0}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}\left|\xi_{1}(s)-\xi_{2}(s)\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}ds\right)^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\xi_{1}(s)-\xi_{2}(s)\right|_{H^{-s^{**}}_{r^{*}}}ds\right)^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\xi_{1}(s)-\xi_{2}(s)\right|^{m^{*}}_{H^{-s^{**}}_{r^{*}}}ds\right)^{\frac{1}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}+C(\varepsilon,T)\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\xi_{1}(s)-\xi_{2}(s)\right|^{m^{*}}_{H^{-s^{**}}_{r^{*}}}ds\right)^{\frac{2}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}+C(\varepsilon,T)\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}.

This implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}I_{0}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}+C(\varepsilon,T)\mathbb{E}\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}+C(\varepsilon,T)\left(\mathbb{E}\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{*}}\right)^{\frac{2}{r^{*}}}.

Let us start with
I_{1}
.
In this we can write

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle=-2\chi\int_{0}^{T}(\nabla^{-1}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)),\nabla^{-1}(\mbox{div}((\xi_{1}(s)-\xi_{2}(s))\nabla c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)))ds

\displaystyle\qquad-2\chi\int_{0}^{T}(\nabla^{-1}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)),\nabla^{-1}(\mbox{div}(\xi_{2}(s)\nabla(c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s))))ds

\displaystyle=I_{1,1}+I_{1,2}.

Let us set for
q>3
,
s^{\ast}=\frac{4}{q+1}=2s^{**}
, and

r=\frac{2q+2}{q},\quad r^{\prime}=\frac{2(q+1)}{q+2},\quad m=\frac{2q+2}{3},\quad m^{\prime}=\frac{2q+2}{2q-1},\quad p=2q-1,\quad p^{\prime}=\frac{2q-1}{2q-2},

Using duality with
m\geq q+1
,
2\leq r<q+1
,
\frac{1}{m}+\frac{1}{m^{\prime}}\leq 1
and
\frac{1}{r}+\frac{1}{r^{\prime}}\leq 1
,
we know that

\displaystyle-2\chi(\nabla^{-1}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)),\nabla^{-1}(\mbox{div}((\xi_{1}(s)-\xi_{2}(s))\nabla c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s))))

\displaystyle=-2\chi\left|(\Delta^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{s^{\ast}}{2}}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)),\Delta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{s^{\ast}}{2}}(\operatorname{div}(\xi_{1}(s)\nabla c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s))-\operatorname{div}(\xi_{2}(s)\nabla c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s))))\right|

\displaystyle\leq-2\chi\left|\Delta^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{s^{\ast}}{2}}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s))\right|_{L^{r}}\times\left|\Delta^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{s^{\ast}}{2}}(\operatorname{div}(\xi_{1}(s)\nabla c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s))-\operatorname{div}(\xi_{2}(s)\nabla c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)))\right|_{L^{r^{\prime}}}.

Integration in time and the Hölder inequality gives
for
\frac{1}{m}+\frac{1}{m^{\prime}}\leq 1

I_{1,1}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\leq\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|^{m}_{H^{{s^{\ast}}-1}_{r}}\,ds\right)^{\frac{m^{*}}{mr^{*}}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|(\xi_{1}(s)-\xi_{2}(s))\nabla c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)\right|^{m^{\prime}}_{H^{-{s^{\ast}}}_{r^{\prime}}}\,ds\right)^{\frac{m^{*}}{m^{\prime}r^{*}}}.

In the next step we put

s_{1}=-s^{\ast}/2
and
s_{2}=\frac{2}{r^{\prime}}-\frac{s^{\ast}}{2}:=\frac{q}{q+1}
. We note that
s_{1}<s_{2}
,
s_{2}<\frac{2}{r^{\prime}}
and
s_{1}+s_{2}>\frac{4}{r^{\prime}}-2
. So, we invoke
[
30
, P. 190, Theorem 1 (iii)]
to get

\left|(\xi_{1}(s)-\xi_{2}(s))\nabla c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)\right|^{m^{\prime}}_{H^{-{s^{\ast}}}_{r^{\prime}}}\leq C\,\left|\xi_{1}(s)-\xi_{2}(s)\right|_{H^{s_{1}}_{r^{\prime}}}^{m^{\prime}}\left|\nabla c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)\right|^{m^{\prime}}_{H^{s_{2}}_{r^{\prime}}}.

Then, we know by the Young inequality that for any
\varepsilon>0
there exists a constant
C(\varepsilon)>0
such that

I_{1,1}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\leq\varepsilon\left\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\right\|^{\frac{rm^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{m}(0,{T};{H^{{s^{\ast}}-1}_{r}})}+C(\varepsilon)\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\xi_{1}(s)-\xi_{2}(s)\right|_{H^{s_{1}}_{r^{\prime}}}^{m^{\prime}}\left|\nabla c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)\right|^{m^{\prime}}_{H^{s_{2}}_{r^{\prime}}}\,ds\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}m^{*}}{m^{\prime}r^{*}}}.

Secondly, we apply Proposition
B.1

to tackle the term

\left\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\right\|^{r}_{L^{m}(0,{\tau_{2\kappa}^{1}(\xi_{2})};{H^{{s^{\ast}}-1}_{r}})}.

In particular,
since

(i)\quad\frac{1}{r}\geq\frac{1}{m}+\frac{1-{s^{\ast}}}{2}

is satisfied,
there exists a constant
C>0
such that

\big\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\big\|^{r}_{L^{m}(0,T;H^{s^{\ast}-1}_{r})}\leq C\left(\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}\right).

Note, that both terms will appear at the left hand side. Thus,
if
\varepsilon
is chosen small enough, we can cancel the terms on the right hand side on the left.
To tackle the second term, i.e.

\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\xi_{1}(s)-\xi_{2}(s)\right|_{H^{s_{1}}_{r^{\prime}}}^{m^{\prime}}\left|\nabla c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)\right|^{m^{\prime}}_{H^{s_{2}}_{r^{\prime}}}\,ds\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{m^{\prime}}}

we apply first the Hölder inequality with

\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}\leq 1
and obtain

\displaystyle I_{1,1}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\varepsilon)\left\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\right\|_{L^{m^{\prime}p}(0,T;H^{-{s^{**}}}_{r^{\prime}})}^{\frac{r^{\prime}m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\|\nabla c_{\kappa}^{(1)}\|_{L^{m^{\prime}p^{\prime}}(0,T;H^{s_{2}}_{r^{\prime}})}^{\frac{r^{\prime}m^{*}}{r^{*}}}.

Note, that to get the stated continuity of item (i) in the norm of
{{\mathcal{M}}}^{m^{\ast}}(0,T;\mathbb{X})
we obtain the necessary condition

(ii)\quad r^{\prime}\leq r^{*}\text{ and }m^{\prime}p=2q+2\leq m^{*}.

However, due to our choice of
r^{\prime},m^{\prime},p
and
s^{\ast}
, this estimate is satisfied.
Thus, we have by the definition of
\mathbb{X}

\left\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\right\|_{L^{m^{\prime}p}(0,T;H^{-{s^{**}}/2}_{r^{\prime}})}\leq C\left\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{X}}.

Since
r^{\prime}<2
and
q>3
, we derive that
m^{\prime}p^{\prime}=\frac{q+1}{q-1}<2
as well as
L^{2}(0,T;H^{1}_{2}({{\mathcal{O}}}))\hookrightarrow L^{m^{\prime}p^{\prime}}(0,T;H^{s_{2}}_{2}({{\mathcal{O}}}))
and therefore since
\frac{r^{\prime}}{2-r^{\prime}}=q+1
, by the Hölder inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}I_{1,1}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\mathbb{E}C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\varepsilon)\mathbb{E}\left\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{\frac{r^{\prime}m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{1+s_{2}}_{2})}^{\frac{r^{\prime}m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\varepsilon)\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{\frac{2r^{\prime}m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}\|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{1+s_{2}}_{2})}^{2r^{\prime}\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\varepsilon)\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{*}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{r^{*}}}\left(\mathbb{E}\|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{1+s_{2}}_{2})}^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{4}}.

In the last line, we have used the fact that
r^{\prime}<2
and
\frac{2r^{\prime}}{r^{*}}=\frac{4(q+1)}{r^{*}(q+2)}<1
since
r^{*}\geq q>4
.

By the interpolation inequality, we derive that

\left|c_{\kappa}\right|_{H_{2}^{1+s_{2}}}\leq C\left|c_{\kappa}\right|_{L^{2}}^{\theta}\left|c_{\kappa}\right|_{H_{2}^{2}}^{1-\theta},

with
\theta:=\frac{1-\varepsilon}{2}=\frac{1}{2q+2}
and
1-\theta=\frac{2q+1}{2q+2}
.
This implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{1+s_{2}}_{2})}^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)\right|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{(q+1)r^{*}}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{2}}^{2}ds\right)^{\frac{(2q+1)m^{*}}{(q+1)r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)\right|_{L^{2}}^{2\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{q+1}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{2}}^{2}ds\right)^{\frac{(2q+1)m^{*}}{qr^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{q}{q+1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{1}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{q+1}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{2}}^{2}ds\right)^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{2q+1}{4q+4}}

\displaystyle\leq C(R).

In the last line, we have used the fact that
\frac{2q+1}{q}<4
and the Sobolev embedding
H^{1}_{2}({{\mathcal{O}}})\hookrightarrow L^{2}({{\mathcal{O}}})
.

Therefore, we can infer that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}I_{1,1}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\varepsilon,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\varepsilon,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{*}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{r^{*}}}.

Let us analyse the term
I_{1,2}
. Reasoning in a very similar way like we have done for
I_{1,1}
, we derive that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}I_{1,2}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\varepsilon)\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|\xi_{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{*}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{r^{*}}}\left(\mathbb{E}\|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{1+s_{2}}_{2})}^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle+C(\varepsilon)\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|\xi_{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{*}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{r^{*}}}\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{L^{2}}^{2\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{4(q+1)}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{2}}^{2}ds\right)^{\frac{(2q+1)m^{*}}{qr^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{qr^{\prime}}{4(q+1)}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\varepsilon,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{L^{2}}^{2\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{qr^{\prime}}{4(q+1)}}.

Here, we have used the fact that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{2}}^{2}ds\right)^{\frac{(2q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\leq\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{2}}^{2}ds\right)^{\frac{4m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{2q+1}{4q+4}},

and

\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{2}}^{2}ds\right)^{\frac{4m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\leq C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{2}}^{2}ds\right)^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}+C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{2}}^{2}ds\right)^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\leq C(R).

Summarising,
we
have shown

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}I_{1}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\varepsilon,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{*}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{r^{*}}}+C(\varepsilon,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{L^{2}}^{2\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{4(q+1)}}.

(3.47)

Next, let us calculate the difference
I_{2}
. In particular, the difference
can be split as follows

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}I_{2}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}|\langle\nabla^{-1}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)),\nabla^{-1}(\mathbf{u}^{(1)}_{\kappa}(s)(\nabla\xi_{1}(s)-\nabla\xi_{2}(s))\rangle|\,ds\right)^{\frac{m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}

\displaystyle{}+C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}|\langle\nabla^{-1}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)),\nabla^{-1}(\nabla\xi_{2}(s)(\mathbf{u}^{(1)}_{\kappa}(s)-\mathbf{u}^{(2)}_{\kappa}(s))\rangle|\,ds\right)^{\frac{m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}

\displaystyle=I_{2,1}+I_{2,2}.

To tackle the first summand, we write

\displaystyle I_{2,1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\,\left(\int_{0}^{T}|\nabla^{-1}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s))|_{H^{s^{\ast}}_{r}}^{m}\,ds\right)^{\frac{1}{m}\frac{m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}|\nabla^{-1}(\mathbf{u}^{(1)}_{\kappa}(s)(\nabla\xi_{1}(s)-\nabla\xi_{2}(s))|_{H^{-s^{\ast}}_{r^{\prime}}}^{m^{\prime}}\,ds\right)^{\frac{1}{m^{\prime}}\frac{m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|_{L^{m}(0,T;{H^{-(1-s^{\ast})}_{r}})}^{r\frac{m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}+C(\varepsilon)\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}|\mathbf{u}^{(1)}_{\kappa}(s)(\nabla\xi_{1}(s)-\nabla\xi_{2}(s))|_{H^{-s^{\ast}-1}_{r^{\prime}}}^{m^{\prime}}\,ds\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{m^{\prime}}\frac{m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}.

Now, we set
s_{1}=-1-s^{\ast}/2
and
s_{2}=1+\delta
, with
\delta:=\frac{2}{r^{\prime}}-\frac{s^{*}}{2}=\frac{q}{q+1}
such that
s_{2}<2
. Since
-1-\frac{s^{*}}{2}>-1-s^{*}
, the following embedding holds
H^{s_{1}}_{r^{\prime}}({{\mathcal{O}}})\hookrightarrow H^{-1-s^{*}}_{r^{\prime}}({{\mathcal{O}}})
. By the fact that
q>4
, we have
s_{1}+s_{2}>\frac{4}{r^{\prime}}-2
,
s_{2}>\frac{2}{r^{\prime}}
and therefore, applying
[
30
, p. 190, Theorem 1 (i)]
with
s_{1}
and
s_{2}
we obtain

\displaystyle|(\mathbf{u}_{1}(s)\nabla\xi_{2}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{2}(s)\nabla\xi_{2}(s))|_{H^{-s^{\ast}-1}_{r^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\leq C|(\mathbf{u}_{1}(s)\nabla\xi_{2}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{2}(s)\nabla\xi_{2}(s))|_{H^{s_{1}}_{r^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\nabla\xi_{1}(s)-\nabla\xi_{2}(s)|_{H^{s_{1}}_{r^{\prime}}}|\mathbf{u}_{1}(s)|_{H^{s_{2}}_{r^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\xi_{1}(s)-\xi_{2}(s)|_{H^{-s^{*}/2}_{r^{\prime}}}|\mathbf{u}_{1}(s)|_{H^{s_{2}}_{r^{\prime}}}.

Hence, we get by the Hölder inequality for
p
and
p^{\prime}
that

\displaystyle I_{2,1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\qquad{}+C(\varepsilon)\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{(1)}_{\kappa}\right\|_{L^{m^{\prime}p^{\prime}}(0,T;H^{s_{2}}_{2})}^{\frac{r^{\prime}m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\|_{L^{m^{\prime}p}(0,T;H^{-s^{**}}_{r^{\prime}})}^{m^{\ast}\frac{r^{\prime}}{r^{\ast}}}.

By the fact that
m^{\prime}p^{\prime}<2
,
m^{\prime}p\leq m^{*}
,
r^{\prime}<2
,
\frac{2r^{\prime}}{r^{*}}=\frac{4(q+1)}{r^{*}(q+2)}<1
(since
r^{*}\geq q\geq 4
) and
s_{2}<2
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{(1)}_{\kappa}\right\|_{L^{m^{\prime}p^{\prime}}(0,T;H^{s_{2}}_{2})}^{\frac{r^{\prime}m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\|_{L^{m^{\prime}p}(0,T;H^{-s^{**}}_{r^{\prime}})}^{m^{\ast}\frac{r^{\prime}}{r^{\ast}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{(1)}_{\kappa}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{2}_{2})}^{\frac{r^{\prime}m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\|_{L^{m^{*}}(0,T;H^{-s^{**}}_{r^{\prime}})}^{m^{\ast}\frac{r^{\prime}}{r^{\ast}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{(1)}_{\kappa}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{2}_{2})}^{\frac{2r^{\prime}m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{\ast}\frac{2r^{\prime}}{r^{\ast}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{(1)}_{\kappa}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{2}_{2})}^{\frac{4m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{4}}\left(\mathbb{E}\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{\ast}\frac{2r^{\prime}}{r^{\ast}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C(R)\left(\mathbb{E}\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{\ast}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{r^{*}}}.

This implies that

I_{2,1}\leq\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}+C(\varepsilon,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{\ast}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{r^{*}}}.

In a similar way like for
I_{2,1}
, we have

\displaystyle I_{2,2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\qquad{}+C(\varepsilon)\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}\right\|_{L^{m^{\prime}p^{\prime}}(0,T;H^{s_{2}}_{2})}^{\frac{r^{\prime}m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}\|\xi_{2}\|_{L^{m^{\prime}p}(0,T;H^{-s^{**}}_{r^{\prime}})}^{m^{\ast}\frac{r^{\prime}}{r^{\ast}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\qquad{}+C(\varepsilon)\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{(1)}_{\kappa}-\mathbf{u}^{(2)}_{\kappa}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{1+\delta}_{2})}^{\frac{4m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{4}}\left(\mathbb{E}\|\xi_{2}\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{\ast}\frac{2r^{\prime}}{r^{\ast}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\qquad{}+C(\varepsilon)\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|\xi_{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{*}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{r^{*}}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{1+\delta}_{2})}^{\frac{4m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{4}}.

By an interpolation inequality (see, e.g.
[
30
]
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|^{2}_{H_{1}^{1+\delta}}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H^{1}_{2}}^{2-2\delta}\int_{0}^{T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H^{2}_{2}}^{2\delta}ds

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{{\bf H}}^{2-2\delta}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{{\bf V}}^{2}ds\right)^{\delta}.

This implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{1+\delta}_{2})}^{\frac{4m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{{\bf H}}^{\frac{4m^{\ast}}{(q+1)r^{\ast}}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{{\bf V}}^{2}ds\right)^{\frac{2qm^{\ast}}{(q+1)r^{\ast}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{{\bf H}}^{\frac{4m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{q+1}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{{\bf V}}^{2}ds\right)^{\frac{2m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}\right)^{\frac{q}{q+1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{{\bf H}}^{\frac{4m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{q+1}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)\right|_{{\bf V}}^{2}ds\right)^{\frac{2m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}+\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)\right|_{{\bf V}}^{2}ds\right)^{\frac{2m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}\right)^{\frac{q}{q+1}}

\displaystyle\leq C(R)\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{{\bf H}}^{\frac{4m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{q+1}}.

From this, we deduce that

\displaystyle I_{2,2}\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

(3.48)

\displaystyle\qquad{}+C(\varepsilon,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{{\bf H}}^{\frac{4m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{4q+4}}.

In sum, we have shown that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}I_{2}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\varepsilon,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{*}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{r^{*}}}+C(\varepsilon,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{{\bf H}}^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{4q+4}}.

(3.49)

By the Burkholder-Gundy-Davis inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}I_{3}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|\int_{0}^{t}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s),g_{\gamma_{1}}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s))dW_{1}(s))_{H_{2}^{-1}}\right|^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\right|^{2}_{H^{-1}_{2}}\left|g_{\gamma_{1}}(n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s))\right|^{2}_{L_{\text{HS}}(H_{1},H_{2}^{-1})}ds\right)^{\frac{m^{*}}{2r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\right|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{H^{-1}_{2}}+C(\varepsilon)\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|g_{\gamma_{1}}(n_{\kappa}(s))\right|^{2}_{L_{\text{HS}}(H_{1},H_{2}^{-1})}ds\right)^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\right|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{H^{-1}_{2}}+C(\varepsilon)\mathbb{E}\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}ds.

By the Hölder inequality, we have

\mathbb{E}I_{4}^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\leq C(m^{*},r^{*},T)\mathbb{E}\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}ds.

Collecting altogether we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(t)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(t)|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}+\left(\frac{r_{n}}{2^{q-1}}\right)^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}ds\right)^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\varepsilon C\|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}\|^{\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\varepsilon,T)\left(\mathbb{E}\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{*}}\right)^{\frac{2}{r^{*}}}+C(\varepsilon,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{*}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\varepsilon,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{1}}^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{2-r^{\prime}}{4}}+C(\varepsilon,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{{\bf H}}^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{4q+4}}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\varepsilon,m^{*},r^{*},T)\mathbb{E}\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}ds.

Taking
\varepsilon<\mu:=\min(1,C^{-1}\left(\frac{r_{n}}{2^{q-1}}\right)^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}})
the term can be cancelled by the left hand side and we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(t)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(t)|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}+\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}ds\right)^{\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,\mu,T)\left(\mathbb{E}\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{*}}\right)^{\frac{2}{r^{*}}}+C(\varepsilon,\mu,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\left\|\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m^{*}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{r^{*}}}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\varepsilon,\mu,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|c_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-c_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{L^{2}}^{2\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{qr^{\prime}}{4(q+1)}}+C(\varepsilon,\mu,R)\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{{\bf H}}^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{r^{\prime}}{4q+4}}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\varepsilon,m^{*},r^{*},\mu,T)\mathbb{E}\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}^{(1)}(s)-n_{\kappa}^{(2)}(s)\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}ds.

Applying the Gronwall inequality, we obtain
the assertion.

∎

d
) of Theorem
2.2
:

{{\mathcal{V}}}_{{\mathfrak{A}}}^{\kappa}
restricted to
\mathcal{X}_{\mathfrak{A}}(R)
, i.e. assumption (

) of Theorem
2.2
.
Here we use the characterisation from Dubinsky, see Theorem IV.1 in Chapter
4
of
[

]
. For this aim, we first recall from Lemma 2.1 in
[
33
]
the following useful result where the proof is given in Appendix A of
[
3
]
.

Lemma 3.9
.

Let
g\in L^{1}(0,T;\mathbb{R})
and
\delta<2
,
\delta\neq 1
. Then

\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{T}\left|t-s\right|^{-\delta}\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}g(r)\,dr\,dt\,ds<\infty,

where
t\vee s=\max(t,s)
and
t\wedge s=\min(t,s)
.

Next we fix
0<s_{0}<s^{**}
. Applying Theorem 2 of
[
30
, p. 82]
with
\varepsilon=s^{**}-s_{0}
, we derive that
5

, we infer that

Now, we prove the following Claim and infer that condition (
d
) of Theorem
2.2
holds by setting
\mathbb{X}^{\prime}=L^{m^{*}}(0,T;H_{r^{*}}^{-s_{0}}({{\mathcal{O}}}))\cap C^{0,\frac{1}{12}}(0,T;H_{2}^{-1}({{\mathcal{O}}}))
and
m_{0}=4
.

Claim 3.10
.

For any
R>0
,
\kappa\in\mathbb{N}
and
\xi\in\mathcal{X}(R)
, there exists a constant
C(\kappa,R)>0
such that

\displaystyle(i)\quad\mathbb{E}\left\|n_{\kappa}\right\|^{4}_{L^{m^{\ast}}(0,T;H_{r^{*}}^{-s_{0}})}\leq C\quad\mbox{and}\quad(ii)\quad\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}\|_{C^{0,\frac{1}{12}}(0,T;H_{2}^{-1})}^{4}\leq C.

where
n_{\kappa}:=\mathcal{V}_{\mathfrak{A}}^{\kappa}(\xi)
.

Proof.

Note, that since
s_{0}<s^{**}
, we have

\frac{1}{r^{*}}\geq\frac{1}{m^{\ast}}+\frac{s^{**}}{2}>\frac{1}{m^{\ast}}+\frac{s_{0}}{2}.

Applying Proposition
B.1
, we derive the existence of a constant
C>0
such that

\|n_{\kappa}\|^{r^{*}}_{L^{m^{\ast}}(0,T;H_{r^{*}}^{-s_{0}})}\leq C\left(\|n_{\kappa}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+\|n_{\kappa}\|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}\right).

In addition, since
m^{*}>2
, due to the estimate (
3.28
), there exists some
C(\kappa,R)>0
such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}|n_{\kappa}|^{4}_{L^{m^{\ast}}(0,T;H_{r^{*}}^{-s_{0}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}\|^{\frac{8}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}+C\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}\|^{(q+1)\frac{4}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}\|^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}\right)^{\frac{2}{m^{*}}}+C\left(\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}\|^{2(q+1)\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}\right)^{\frac{2}{m^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\kappa,R).

This gives the compact containment condition (i).

To prove the estimate (ii), since
\frac{1}{3}\times 4>1
and
\frac{1}{12}=\frac{1}{3}-\frac{1}{4}
, we derive from
[
9
, Page 372 and 376]
the following embedding

W^{\frac{1}{3},4}(0,T;H_{2}^{-1}({{\mathcal{O}}}))\hookrightarrow C^{0,\frac{1}{12}}(0,T;H_{2}^{-1}({{\mathcal{O}}})).

(3.50)

This implies that

\mathbb{E}|n_{\kappa}|^{4}_{C^{0,\frac{1}{12}}(0,T;H_{2}^{-1})}\leq C\mathbb{E}\left\|n_{\kappa}\right\|^{4}_{W^{\frac{1}{3},4}(0,T;H_{2}^{-1})}.

(3.51)

Since
m^{*}>r^{*}
, due to the Hölder inequality and the estimate (
3.28
), we note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|n_{\kappa}\right\|^{4}_{W^{\frac{1}{3},4}(0,T;H_{2}^{-1})}

\displaystyle:=\mathbb{E}\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{-1}}^{4}ds+\mathbb{E}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{T}\,{|n_{\kappa}(t)-n_{\kappa}(s)|_{H_{2}^{-1}}^{4}\over|t-s|^{1+\frac{4}{3}}}\,ds\,dt

\displaystyle\leq CT\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{-1}}^{4}ds+\mathbb{E}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{T}\,{|n_{\kappa}(t)-n_{\kappa}(s)|_{H_{2}^{-1}}^{4}\over|t-s|^{\frac{7}{3}}}\,ds\,dt

\displaystyle\leq CT\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|_{H_{2}^{-1}}^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}ds\right)^{\frac{r^{*}}{m^{*}}}+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{T}\,{\mathbb{E}|n_{\kappa}(t)-n_{\kappa}(s)|_{H_{2}^{-1}}^{4}\over|t-s|^{\frac{7}{3}}}\,ds\,dt

\displaystyle\leq C(\kappa,R)+J.

From the equation (
3.34
), we derive that for any
t,s\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}|n_{\kappa}(t)-n_{\kappa}(s)|_{H_{2}^{-1}}^{4}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|\Delta|n_{\kappa}(r)|^{q-1}n_{\kappa}(r)\right|_{H_{2}^{-1}}dr\right)^{4}+C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|\xi(r)\right|_{H_{2}^{-1}}dr\right)^{4}

\displaystyle\quad+C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|\mbox{div}(\xi(r)\nabla c_{\kappa}(r))\right|_{H_{2}^{-1}}dr\right)^{4}+C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(r)\cdot\nabla\xi(r)\right|_{H_{2}^{-1}}dr\right)^{4}

\displaystyle\quad+C\mathbb{E}\left|\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}g_{\gamma_{3}}(n_{\kappa}(r))dW_{1}(r)\right|^{4}_{H_{2}^{-1}}.

By using the fact that
H^{1}_{2}({{\mathcal{O}}})\hookrightarrow L^{q+1}({{\mathcal{O}}})
, we derive that
L^{\frac{q+1}{q}}({{\mathcal{O}}})\hookrightarrow H^{-1}_{2}({{\mathcal{O}}})
and therefore,

\left|\Delta|n_{\kappa}|^{q-1}n_{\kappa}\right|_{H_{2}^{-1}}\leq C\left|\Delta|n_{\kappa}|^{q-1}n_{\kappa}\right|_{L^{\frac{q+1}{q}}}.

We recall that the duality
{}_{L^{\frac{q+1}{q}}}\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{L^{q+1}}
is given by the equality (
3.36
). So, for any
w\in L^{q+1}({{\mathcal{O}}})
,

{}_{L^{\frac{q+1}{q}}}\langle\Delta|n_{\kappa}|^{q-1}n_{\kappa},w\rangle_{L^{q+1}}

\displaystyle=\int_{{\mathcal{O}}}|n_{\kappa}(x)|^{q-1}n_{\kappa}(x)w(x)dx

\displaystyle\leq\int_{{\mathcal{O}}}|n_{\kappa}(x)|^{q}\left|w(x)\right|dx

\displaystyle\leq\left|n_{\kappa}\right|_{L^{q+1}}\left|w\right|_{L^{q+1}}.

This implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|\Delta|n_{\kappa}(r)|^{q-1}n_{\kappa}(r)\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}dr\right)^{4}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|n_{\kappa}(r)\right|_{L^{q+1}}dr\right)^{4}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|n_{\kappa}(r)\right|^{2}_{L^{q+1}}dr\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}ds\right)^{\frac{4}{q+1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}ds\right)^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{2r^{*}}{(q+1)m^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C(k,R)\left|t-s\right|^{2}.

For the two last lines, we have used the Hölder inequality (since
\frac{(q+1)m^{*}}{2r^{*}}>1
) and the estimate (
3.28
).
Using the embeddings
L^{q+1}({{\mathcal{O}}})\hookrightarrow L^{\frac{q+1}{q}}({{\mathcal{O}}})\hookrightarrow H^{-1}_{2}({{\mathcal{O}}})
and the Hölder inequality, we derive that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|\xi(r)\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}dr\right)^{4}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|\xi(r)\right|^{2}_{L^{q+1}}dr\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\xi(r)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}dr\right)^{\frac{4}{q+1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\xi(s)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}ds\right)^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{2r^{*}}{(q+1)m^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C(k,R)\left|t-s\right|^{2}.

Interpolating
L^{4}({{\mathcal{O}}})
between
H^{1}_{2}({{\mathcal{O}}})
and
H^{2}_{2}({{\mathcal{O}}})
in two dimensional cases, and using the embedding of
L^{q+1}({{\mathcal{O}}})
into
L^{4}({{\mathcal{O}}})
, we infer that

\displaystyle\left|\mbox{div}(\xi\nabla c_{\kappa})\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\xi\right|_{L^{4}}\left|\nabla c\right|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\xi\right|_{L^{q+1}}\left|c_{\kappa}\right|_{H^{1}_{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left|c_{\kappa}\right|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{H_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\xi\right|_{L^{q+1}}^{2}+C\left|c_{\kappa}\right|\left|c_{\kappa}\right|_{H_{2}^{2}}.

This implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|\mbox{div}(\xi(r)\nabla c_{\kappa}(r))\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}dr\right)^{4}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|\xi(r)\right|^{2}_{L^{q+1}}dr\right)^{4}+C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|c_{\kappa}(r)\right|_{H^{1}_{2}}\left|c_{\kappa}(r)\right|_{H_{2}^{2}}dr\right)^{4}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|\xi(r)\right|^{4}_{L^{q+1}}dr\right)^{2}+C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|c_{\kappa}(r)\right|_{H^{1}_{2}}^{2}\left|c_{\kappa}(r)\right|_{H_{2}^{2}}^{2}dr\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\xi(r)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}dr\right)^{\frac{8}{q+1}}+C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|_{H^{1}_{2}}^{4}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}(r)\right|^{2}_{H_{2}^{2}}dr\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\xi(s)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}ds\right)^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{4r^{*}}{(q+1)m^{*}}}+C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{H_{2}^{2}}dr\right)^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{r^{*}}{m^{*}}}

\displaystyle\qquad+C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|_{H^{1}_{2}}^{8\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{r^{*}}{m^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\kappa,R)\left|t-s\right|^{2},

where we have used the fact that
q+1>4
, the Hölder inequality and the estimates (
3.28
) and (
3.27
).
In a similar way, after an integration-by-part, we arrive at

\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}\cdot\nabla\xi\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}\leq C\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}\right|_{\mathbf{L}^{4}}\left|\xi\right|_{L^{4}}\leq C\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}\right|_{{\bf H}}\left|\xi\right|_{L^{q+1}}\leq C\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}\right|_{{\bf H}}^{2}+C\left|\xi\right|^{2}_{L^{q+1}},

which implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(r)\cdot\nabla\xi(r)\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}dr\right)^{4}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(r)\right|_{{\bf H}}^{2}dr\right)^{4}+C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}C\left|\xi(r)\right|^{2}_{L^{q+1}}dr\right)^{4}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(r)\right|_{{\bf H}}^{4}dr\right)^{2}+C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}C\left|\xi(r)\right|^{4}_{L^{q+1}}dr\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(r)\right|_{{\bf H}}^{8}+C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\xi(r)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}dr\right)^{\frac{8}{q+1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(r)\right|_{{\bf H}}^{8\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{r^{*}}{m^{*}}}+C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\xi(s)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}ds\right)^{\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{4r^{*}}{(q+1)m^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\kappa,R)\left|t-s\right|^{2},

where the estimates (
3.28
) and (
3.26
) have been used.
By the Burkholder-Gundy-Davis inequality and inequality (
3.17
), we derive that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}g_{\gamma_{3}}(n_{\kappa}(r))dW_{1}(r)\right|^{4}_{H^{-1}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|g_{\gamma_{3}}(n_{\kappa}(r))\right|^{2}_{L_{HS}(H_{1},H^{-1}_{2})}dr\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}\left|g_{\gamma_{3}}(n_{\kappa}(r))\right|^{4}_{L_{HS}(H_{1},H^{-1}_{2})}dr

\displaystyle\leq C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|n_{\kappa}(r)\right|^{4}_{H^{-1}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|t-s\right|^{2}\left(\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|n_{\kappa}(r)\right|^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}_{H^{-1}_{2}}\right)^{\frac{r^{*}}{m^{*}}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\kappa,R)\left|t-s\right|^{2}.

From these inequalities, we infer that

\mathbb{E}|n_{\kappa}(t)-n_{\kappa}(s)|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{4}\leq C(\kappa,R)\left|t-s\right|^{2},

(3.52)

and therefore by the fact that
\left|t-s\right|=\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}dr
, we arrive at

\displaystyle J

\displaystyle\leq C(\kappa,R)\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{T}|t-s|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\,ds\,dt=C(\kappa,R)\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{T}|t-s|^{-\frac{4}{3}}\int_{t\wedge s}^{t\vee s}drds\,dt<\infty.

Here we have applied Lemma
3.9
with
\delta=\frac{4}{3}
and
g(r)=1
for all
r\in[0,T]
. Since

\mathbb{E}\left\|n_{\kappa}\right\|^{4}_{W^{\frac{1}{3},4}(0,T;H^{-1}_{2})}\leq C(\kappa,R)+J,

then the condition (ii) holds and the claim is proven showing that condition (
d
) is satisfied.
∎

Verification of assumption (
e
) of Theorem
2.2
:

Here we will find
m_{1}>m^{*}
such that
\mathbb{E}\|{{\mathcal{V}}}_{{\mathfrak{A}}}^{\kappa}(\xi)\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{m_{1}}\leq C(\kappa,R)
for all
\xi\in{\mathcal{X}}^{\kappa}_{{\mathfrak{A}}}(R)
. For this aim, let us set
m_{1}=2m^{*}
. Since
2m^{\ast}>q+1
,
2<r^{\ast}<q+1
and
s^{\ast\ast}\in(0,1)
, we have
\frac{2m^{\ast}}{r^{\ast}}>1
. By applying Proposition
B.1
and invoking the inequality (
3.28
), we get for any
\xi\in{\mathcal{X}}^{\kappa}_{{\mathfrak{A}}}(R)

\mathbb{E}\|n_{\kappa}(\xi)\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{2m^{\ast}}=\mathbb{E}\left(\|n_{\kappa}(\xi)\|_{\mathbb{X}}^{r^{*}}\right)^{2\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\leq C\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}|n_{\kappa}(\xi)(s)|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{4\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}+C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}|n_{\kappa}(\xi)(s)|_{L^{q+1}}^{q+1}\,ds\right)^{2\frac{m^{*}}{r^{*}}}\leq C(\kappa,R).

It remains to verify assumption (
f
) which is the path-wise continuity of
n_{\kappa}
,
c_{\kappa}
and
\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}
.

Verification of condition (
f
) of Theorem
2.2
:
Since the inequality (
3.52
) holds, by the Kolmogorov Continuity Theorem,

{\mathbb{P}}
-a.s.
n_{\kappa}\in C(0,T;H^{-1}_{2}({{\mathcal{O}}}))
. In the same way we can also prove that
{\mathbb{P}}
-a.s.
c_{\kappa}\in C(0,T;L^{2}({{\mathcal{O}}}))
and
\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}\in C(0,T;\mathbf{L}^{2}({{\mathcal{O}}}))
and the condition (
f
) follows.

Verification of the inequality (
2.21
):
Here, we will prove that inequality (
2.21
) holds. To be more precise, assuming that
(n_{\kappa},c_{\kappa},\mathbf{u}_{\kappa})
is a fixed point of system (
2
), we will give a uniform bound independently on
\kappa
. Then, we will consider the following system

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcl}d{n}_{\kappa}(t)&=&\left(r_{n}\Delta|n_{\kappa}(t)|^{q-1}n_{\kappa}(t)+\theta n_{\kappa}(t)-\chi\mbox{div}(n_{\kappa}(t)\nabla c_{\kappa}(t))\right)\,dt\\
&&{}+\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(t)\cdot\nabla n_{\kappa}(t)\,dt+g_{\gamma_{1}}(n_{\kappa}(t))dW_{1}(t),\\
d{c_{\kappa}}(t)&=&\left(r_{c}\Delta c_{\kappa}(t)-\alpha c_{\kappa}(t)+\beta n_{\kappa}(t)\right)\,dt\\
&&{}+\Theta_{\kappa}(\mathbf{u}_{\kappa},t)\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(t)\cdot\nabla c_{\kappa}(t)\,dt+g_{\gamma_{2}}(c_{\kappa}(t))dW_{2}(t),\\
d\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(t)&=&r_{\mathbf{u}}\Delta\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(t)dt+n_{\kappa}(t)\star\Phi dt+\sigma_{\gamma_{3}}dW_{3}(t),\end{array}\right.

and prove the following claim.

Claim 3.11
.

For any
p\in[1,\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}]
, there exist a constant
C=C(p,q,T)
such that for all
\kappa\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{\bf H}+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|c_{k}(s)\right|^{2p}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}|n_{\kappa}(s)|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2p}

\displaystyle+r^{p}_{n}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}ds\right)^{p}+\min(r_{c}^{p},\alpha^{p})\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{H^{1}_{2}}ds\right)^{p}+r^{p}_{u}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{\bf V}ds\right)^{p}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\mathbb{E}\left|\mathbf{u}_{0}\right|^{2p}_{\bf H}+\mathbb{E}\left|c_{0}\right|^{2p}_{L^{2}}+\mathbb{E}|n_{0}|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2p}+1\right).

Proof.

By the Itô formula to the process
t\longmapsto\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(t)\right|^{2}_{\bf H}
we can derive that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{\bf H}+r_{u}\int_{0}^{T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{\bf V}ds

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathbf{u}_{0}\right|^{2}_{\bf H}+C(\delta_{1},\delta_{2})\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{H^{-1}_{2}}ds+T\left|\sigma_{\gamma_{3}}\right|^{2}_{L_{HS}(H,{\bf H})}

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{{\bf H}}ds+C\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|\int_{0}^{t}(\nabla\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s),\nabla\sigma_{\gamma_{3}})dW_{3}(s)\right|.

(3.59)

Next, since
(\Theta_{\kappa}(\mathbf{u}_{\kappa},s)\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\cdot\nabla c_{\kappa}(s),c_{k}(s))_{L^{2}}=0
, by the Itô formula to the process
t\longmapsto\left|c_{\kappa}(t)\right|_{L^{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|c_{k}(s)\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2\min(r_{c},\alpha)\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{H^{1}_{2}}ds

\displaystyle\leq\left|c_{0}\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2\beta\int_{0}^{T}\left|(n_{\kappa}(s),c_{k}(s))_{L^{2}}\right|ds+\int_{0}^{T}\left|g_{\gamma_{2}}(c_{\kappa}(s))\right|^{2}_{L_{HS}(H_{1};L^{2})}ds

\displaystyle\quad+2\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|\int_{0}^{t}(c_{k}(s),g_{\gamma_{2}}(c_{\kappa}(s))dW_{2}(s))_{L^{2}}\right|

Note, by the Young inequality, we know that

\displaystyle\beta(n_{\kappa},c_{\kappa})_{L^{2}}\leq|n_{\kappa}|_{H^{-1}_{2}}|c_{\kappa}|_{H^{1}_{2}}\leq\frac{\min(r_{c},\alpha)}{2}|c_{\kappa}|^{2}_{H^{1}_{2}}+C|n_{\kappa}|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}.

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\left|g_{\gamma_{2}}(c_{\kappa})\right|^{2}_{L_{HS}(H_{1};L^{2})}\leq C|c_{\kappa}|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

This implies that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|c_{k}(s)\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{3\min(r_{c},\alpha)}{2}\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{H^{1}_{2}}ds

\displaystyle\leq\left|c_{0}\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}+C\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}ds+C\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{H^{-1}_{2}}ds

\displaystyle\qquad+2\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|\int_{0}^{t}(c_{k}(s),g_{\gamma_{2}}(c_{\kappa}(s))dW_{2}(s))_{L^{2}}\right|.

(3.60)

Let us start with the next entity given by
|n_{\kappa}|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}
. Here we get by the Itô formula

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}|n_{\kappa}(s)|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}+2r_{n}\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}ds

\displaystyle\leq|n_{0}|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}+2\theta\int_{0}^{T}|n_{\kappa}(s)|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}ds+2\chi\int_{0}^{T}\left|(\nabla^{-1}n_{\kappa}(s),\nabla^{-1}(\mbox{div}(n_{\kappa}(s)\nabla c_{\kappa}(s))))\right|ds

(3.61)

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{T}\left|(\nabla^{-1}n_{\kappa}(s),\nabla^{-1}\left({\bf u}_{\kappa}(s)\cdot\nabla n_{\kappa}(s)\right))\right|ds+\int_{0}^{T}|g_{\gamma_{1}}(n_{\kappa}(s))|_{L_{\text{HS}}(H_{1},H_{2}^{-1})}^{2}\,ds

\displaystyle\quad{}+2\sup_{0\leq t\leq{T}}\int_{0}^{t}\left(n_{\kappa}(s),g_{\gamma_{1}}(n_{\kappa}(s))\,dW_{1}(s)\right)_{H^{-1}_{2}}.

Let us analyse term by term.
By the Hölder and Young inequality we know after an integration by-part that

\displaystyle 2\chi\left|(\nabla^{-1}n_{\kappa},\nabla^{-1}(\mbox{div}(n_{\kappa}\nabla c_{\kappa})))\right|

\displaystyle=2\chi\left|(n_{\kappa},\nabla^{-1}(n_{\kappa}\nabla c_{\kappa}))\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\chi\left|n_{\kappa}\right|_{L^{q+1}}\left|\nabla^{-1}(n_{\kappa}\nabla c_{\kappa})\right|_{L^{\frac{q+1}{q}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{n}}{2}\left|n_{\kappa}\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}+C\left|(n_{\kappa}\nabla c_{\kappa})\right|^{\frac{q+1}{q}}_{H^{-1}_{\frac{q+1}{q}}}.

Due to the embedding
L^{1}({{\mathcal{O}}})\hookrightarrow H^{-1}_{\frac{q+1}{q}}({{\mathcal{O}}})
(see,
[
30
, P. 82, Theorem 1 (i)]
), and the Hölder and Young inequality, we infer that that

\displaystyle\left|(\nabla^{-1}n_{\kappa},\nabla^{-1}(\mbox{div}(n_{\kappa}\nabla c_{\kappa})))\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{n}}{2}\left|n_{\kappa}\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}+C\left|(n_{\kappa}\nabla c_{\kappa})\right|^{\frac{q+1}{q}}_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{n}}{4}\left|n_{\kappa}\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}+C\left|n_{\kappa}\right|^{\frac{q+1}{q}}_{L^{q+1}}\left|\nabla c_{\kappa}\right|^{\frac{q+1}{q}}_{L^{\frac{q+1}{q-1}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{n}}{2}\left|n_{\kappa}\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}+C\left|\nabla c_{\kappa}\right|^{\frac{q+1}{q-1}}_{L^{\frac{q+1}{q-1}}}.

Since
\frac{q+1}{q-1}<2
, by the Sobolev embedding and the Young inequality we arrive at

\left|(\nabla^{-1}n_{\kappa},\nabla^{-1}(\mbox{div}(n_{\kappa}\nabla c_{\kappa})))\right|\leq\left|n_{\kappa}\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}+\frac{\min(r_{c},\alpha)}{2}\left|\nabla c_{\kappa}\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}+C(q).

In a very similar way, since
L^{q+1}({{\mathcal{O}}})\hookrightarrow H^{-1}_{2}({{\mathcal{O}}})
,
L^{q+1}({{\mathcal{O}}})\hookrightarrow L^{4}({{\mathcal{O}}})
and
{\bf H}\hookrightarrow\mathbf{L}^{4}({{\mathcal{O}}})
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|(\nabla^{-1}n_{\kappa},\nabla^{-1}\left({\bf u}_{\kappa}\cdot\nabla n_{\kappa}\right))\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|n_{\kappa}\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}\left|{\bf u}_{\kappa}\cdot\nabla n_{\kappa}\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|n_{\kappa}\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}\left|{\bf u}_{\kappa}\right|_{\mathbf{L}^{4}}\left|n_{\kappa}\right|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|n_{\kappa}\right|_{L^{q+1}}^{2}\left|{\bf u}_{\kappa}\right|_{{\bf H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|n_{\kappa}\right|_{L^{q+1}}^{4}+C\left|{\bf u}_{\kappa}\right|^{2}_{{\bf H}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{n}}{2}\left|n_{\kappa}\right|_{L^{q+1}}^{q+1}+C+C\left|{\bf u}_{\kappa}\right|^{2}_{{\bf H}}.

Using the inequality (
3.17
), we get

|g_{\gamma_{1}}(n_{\kappa})|_{L_{\text{HS}}(H_{1},H_{2}^{-1})}\leq C\left|n_{\kappa}\right|_{H^{-1}_{2}}.

Substituting the estimates from above into the inequality (
2
), we get

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}|n_{\kappa}(s)|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}+r_{n}\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}ds

\displaystyle\leq|n_{0}|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}+C\int_{0}^{T}|n_{\kappa}(s)|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}ds+C+\frac{\min(r_{c},\alpha)}{2}\int_{0}^{T}|c_{\kappa}(s)|_{H^{1}_{2}}^{2}ds

(3.62)

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{T}|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)|_{{\bf H}}^{2}ds+2\sup_{0\leq t\leq{T}}\left|\int_{0}^{t}\left(n_{\kappa}(s),g_{\gamma_{1}}(n_{\kappa}(s))\,dW_{1}(s)\right)_{H^{-1}_{2}}\right|.

Adding up the inequalities (
3.59
), (
2
) and (
3.62
) we get

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{\bf H}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|c_{k}(s)\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}|n_{\kappa}(s)|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+r_{n}\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}ds+\min(r_{c},\alpha)\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{H^{1}_{2}}ds+r_{u}\int_{0}^{T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{\bf V}ds

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathbf{u}_{0}\right|^{2}_{\bf H}+\left|c_{0}\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}+|n_{0}|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}+C+C\int_{0}^{T}\left(\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{{\bf H}}+\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{H^{-1}_{2}}\right)ds

(3.63)

\displaystyle\qquad+C\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|\int_{0}^{t}(\nabla\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s),\nabla\sigma_{\gamma_{3}})dW_{3}(s)\right|+2\sup_{0\leq t\leq{T}}\left|\int_{0}^{t}\left(n_{\kappa}(s),g_{\gamma_{1}}(n_{\kappa}(s))\,dW_{1}(s)\right)_{H^{-1}_{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+2\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|\int_{0}^{t}(c_{k}(s),g_{\gamma_{2}}(c_{\kappa}(s))dW_{2}(s))_{L^{2}}\right|.

Let
p\in[1,\frac{2m^{*}}{r^{*}}]
be fixed. By taking the exponent
p
and the expectation in (
3.63
), we derive that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{\bf H}+\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|c_{k}(s)\right|^{2p}_{L^{2}}+\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}|n_{\kappa}(s)|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2p}

\displaystyle+r^{p}_{n}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}ds\right)^{p}+\min(r_{c}^{p},\alpha^{p})\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{H^{1}_{2}}ds\right)^{p}+r^{p}_{u}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{\bf V}ds\right)^{p}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left|\mathbf{u}_{0}\right|^{2p}_{\bf H}+C\mathbb{E}\left|c_{0}\right|^{2p}_{L^{2}}+C\mathbb{E}|n_{0}|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2p}+C+C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left(\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{{\bf H}}+\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{H^{-1}_{2}}\right)ds\right)^{p}

(3.64)

\displaystyle\qquad+C\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|\int_{0}^{t}(\nabla\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s),\nabla\sigma_{\gamma_{3}})dW_{3}(s)\right|^{p}+C\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq{T}}\left|\int_{0}^{t}\left(n_{\kappa}(s),g_{\gamma_{1}}(n_{\kappa}(s))\,dW_{1}(s)\right)_{H^{-1}_{2}}\right|^{p}

\displaystyle\qquad+C\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|\int_{0}^{t}(c_{k}(s),g_{\gamma_{2}}(c_{\kappa}(s))dW_{2}(s))_{L^{2}}\right|^{p}.

By the Hölder inequality, we get

\displaystyle C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left(\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{{\bf H}}+\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{H^{-1}_{2}}\right)ds\right)^{p}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\int_{0}^{T}\left(\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{{\bf H}}+\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{H^{-1}_{2}}\right)^{p}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\int_{0}^{T}\left(\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{{\bf H}}+\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{L^{2}}+\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{H^{-1}_{2}}\right)ds.

By the Burkholder-Gundy-Davis inequality, the Hölder inequality and Young inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle C\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|\int_{0}^{t}(\nabla\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s),\nabla\sigma_{\gamma_{3}})dW_{3}(s)\right|^{p}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\sigma_{\gamma_{3}}\right|^{p}_{L_{HS}(H,{\bf H})}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\nabla\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}}^{2}ds\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\sigma_{\gamma_{3}}\right|^{p}_{L_{HS}(H,{\bf H})}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\nabla\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}}^{2p}ds\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\sigma_{\gamma_{3}}\right|^{p}_{L_{HS}(H,{\bf H})}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\nabla\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|_{\mathbf{L}^{2}}^{2p}ds\right)\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\int_{0}^{T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{\bf H}ds+C\left|\sigma_{\gamma_{3}}\right|^{2p}_{L_{HS}(H,{\bf H})},

and

\displaystyle C\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|\int_{0}^{t}(c_{k}(s),g_{\gamma_{2}}(c_{\kappa}(s))dW_{2}(s))_{L^{2}}\right|^{p}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}\left|g_{\gamma_{2}}(c_{\kappa}(s))\right|^{2}_{L_{HS}(H_{1};L^{2})}ds\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{L^{2}}+C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|g_{\gamma_{2}}(c_{\kappa}(s))\right|^{2}_{L_{HS}(H_{1};L^{2})}ds\right)^{p}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{L^{2}}+C\mathbb{E}\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{L^{2}}ds.

In a very similar way, we also get

\displaystyle C\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|\int_{0}^{t}(n_{k}(s),g_{\gamma_{1}}(n_{\kappa}(s))dW_{1}(s))_{H^{-1}_{2}}\right|^{p}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{H^{-1}_{2}}\left|g_{\gamma_{1}}(n_{\kappa}(s))\right|^{2}_{L_{HS}(H_{1};H^{-1}_{2})}ds\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{H^{-1}_{2}}+C\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|g_{\gamma_{1}}(n_{\kappa}(s))\right|^{2}_{L_{HS}(H_{1};H^{-1}_{2})}ds\right)^{p}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{H^{-1}_{2}}+C\mathbb{E}\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{H^{-1}_{2}}ds.

Using the inequality above, we infer from inequality (
3.64
) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{\bf H}+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\left|c_{k}(s)\right|^{2p}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\sup_{0\leq s\leq{T}}|n_{\kappa}(s)|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2p}

\displaystyle+r^{p}_{n}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}}ds\right)^{p}+\min(r_{c}^{p},\alpha^{p})\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{H^{1}_{2}}ds\right)^{p}+r^{p}_{u}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2}_{\bf V}ds\right)^{p}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left|\mathbf{u}_{0}\right|^{2p}_{\bf H}+C\mathbb{E}\left|c_{0}\right|^{2p}_{L^{2}}+C\mathbb{E}|n_{0}|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2p}+C+C\mathbb{E}\int_{0}^{T}\left(\left|\mathbf{u}_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{{\bf H}}+\left|c_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{L^{2}}+\left|n_{\kappa}(s)\right|^{2p}_{H^{-1}_{2}}\right)ds,

and applying the Gronwall Lemma to that term, we end the proof of the claim.
∎

In particular, the inequality (
2.21
) comes from Claim
3.11
and the definition of cut-off functions given in Step (I).

Step (III)

Having verified the Assumptions of the Theorem
2.2
we know, there exists a martingale solution to system (
3
). Since we have higher order moments of the initial conditions, we can obtain the estimate (
3.4
) using very similar argument like in Claim
3.11
and end the proof of Theorem
3.4
.

∎

Appendix A
Technical Preliminaries of the noise and the stochastic integral

Let
\mathfrak{A}:=(\Omega,{{\mathcal{F}}},{\mathbb{F}},\mathbb{P})
be a complete filtered
probability space with a filtration
{\mathbb{F}}=\{{{\mathcal{F}}}_{t}:t\in[0,T]\}
satisfying the usual conditions and Let
H=L^{2}(\mathcal{O})
.
For a separable Hilbert space
E
,
let
L_{HS}(H,E)
be the space of all Hilbert-Schmidt operators from
H
to
E
.
Let us denote by
{{\mathcal{M}}}^{2}_{\mathfrak{A}}(0,T;E)
the space of all
\mathbb{F}
-progressively measurable stochastic processes
\xi:[0,T]\times\Omega\to E
with

\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}|\xi(s)|^{2}_{E}{}ds\right)<\infty.

For any
\xi\in{{\mathcal{M}}}^{2}_{\mathfrak{A}}(0,T;E)
and
\gamma_{1}
,
\gamma_{2}>0
, let us define the process
M_{\gamma_{j}}=\{M_{\gamma_{j}}(t):t\in[0,T]\}
, given by

\displaystyle M_{\gamma_{j}}(t)=\int_{0}^{t}g_{\gamma_{j}}(\xi(s))dW_{j}(s),\qquad t\in[0,T],\ j=1,2.

For
j=1,2
, the Itô isometry reads

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\left|\int_{0}^{t}g_{\gamma_{j}}(\xi(s)){}dW_{j}(s)\right|_{E}^{2}\right)=\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{t}|g_{\gamma_{j}}(\xi(s))|^{2}_{L_{HS}(H,E)}{}ds\right),\qquad t\in[0,T].

(A.1)

Note that for
q\geq 1
we have the Burkholder–Gundy–Davis inequality (see, e.g,
[
28
]
)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left|\int_{0}^{s}g_{\gamma_{j}}(\xi(r)){}dW(r)\right|_{E}^{q}\right)\leq C_{q}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{t}|g_{\gamma_{j}}(\xi(s))|_{L_{HS}(H,E)}^{2}ds\right)^{\frac{q}{2}},\quad j=1,2.

(A.2)

To show existence and boundedness for the process
n_{\kappa}
or
c_{\kappa}
, we estimate the
L_{HS}(H;L^{2}({{\mathcal{O}}}))
-norm and
L_{HS}(H;H^{-1}_{2}({{\mathcal{O}}}))
-norm of
g_{\gamma_{j}}
depending on the choice of
\gamma_{j}
,
j=2,1
respectively. For this aim we recall from
[
1
]
that the eigenfunctions
\{\varphi_{k}:k\in\mathbb{N}\}
of the Laplace operator
(-\Delta)
on
L^{2}({{\mathcal{O}}})
are constructed such that the following estimate of the asymptotic behaviour of the eigenvalues
\{\lambda_{k}:k\in\mathbb{N}\}
holds:
There exist two numbers
0<c<C
such that

\displaystyle c\cdot k^{\frac{2}{d}}\leq\lambda_{k}\leq C\cdot k^{\frac{2}{d}},\quad\mbox{ for all }k\in\mathbb{N}.

(A.3)

In addition, there exists some constant
c>0
such that

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in{{\mathcal{O}}}}|\varphi_{k}(x)|\leq c\,\lambda_{k}^{\frac{d-1}{2}},\quad k\in\mathbb{N}.

(A.4)

To start with, let
E=L^{2}({{\mathcal{O}}})
.
Due to the inequalities (
A.3
) and (
A.4
), we can write for
u\in L^{2}(\mathcal{O})
and
\gamma_{2}>0
,

\displaystyle|g_{\gamma_{2}}(u)|_{L_{HS}(H,L^{2})}^{2}=\sum_{k}^{\infty}\lambda_{k}^{-\gamma_{2}}|u\varphi_{k}|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C|u|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,\sum_{k}^{\infty}|\varphi_{k}|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\lambda_{k}^{-\gamma_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C|u|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,\sum_{k}^{\infty}\lambda_{k}^{d-1-\gamma_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\,|u|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,\sum_{k}^{\infty}k^{\frac{2}{d}(d-1-\gamma_{2})},

which is finite for
\gamma_{2}>\frac{3d}{2}-1
.
Summarising, we can write for any
\gamma_{2}>\frac{3d}{2}-1
, there exists some
C(\gamma_{2})>0
such that

\displaystyle|g_{\gamma_{2}}(u)|_{L_{HS}(H,L^{2})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C|u|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

(A.5)

Next, let
E=H^{-1}_{2}({{\mathcal{O}}})
,
u\in L^{2}(\mathcal{O})
and
\gamma_{1}>0
. Then we know by the first point of Theorem
B.3
that for
\delta>1

\displaystyle|g_{\gamma_{1}}(u)|_{L_{HS}(H,H^{-1}_{2})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k}^{\infty}\lambda_{k}^{-\gamma_{1}}|u\varphi_{k}|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C^{2}|u|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}\,\sum_{k}^{\infty}|\varphi_{k}|_{H^{\delta}_{2}}^{2}\lambda_{k}^{-\gamma_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq C^{2}|u|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}\,\sum_{k}^{\infty}\lambda_{k}^{\delta-\gamma_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\,|u|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}\,\sum_{k}^{\infty}k^{\frac{2}{d}(\delta-\gamma_{1})},

which is finite for
\gamma_{1}>1+\frac{d}{2}
.
Summerising, we know, for any
\gamma_{1}>1+\frac{d}{2}
there exists some
C(\gamma_{1})>0
with

\displaystyle|g_{\gamma_{1}}(u)|_{L_{HS}(H,H^{-1}_{2})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C|u|_{H^{-1}_{2}}^{2}.

(A.6)

Appendix B
Some technical Proposition

In this section we have collected several technical inequalities, which we need through our proof.

Proposition B.1
.

For any
r\in(2,q+1)
,
m\in(q+1,\infty)
,
s\in(0,1)
, with
\frac{1}{r}\geq\frac{1}{m}+\frac{s}{2}
, there exists a constant
C>0
such that

\displaystyle\|\xi\|_{L^{m}(0,T;H^{-s}_{r})}^{r}\leq C\left(\|\xi\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H_{2}^{-1})}+\|\xi\|^{q+1}_{L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1})}\right).

(B.1)

Proof.

To be for

L^{m}(0,T;H^{-s}_{r}({{\mathcal{O}}}))
an
interpolation space between

L^{\infty}(0,T;H_{2}^{-1}({{\mathcal{O}}}))\cap L^{q+1}(0,T;L^{q+1}({{\mathcal{O}}}))

we need, that the parameters
m,r,s
satisfies the following inequalities:

\displaystyle-s=-\theta+(1-\theta)0,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}=\frac{\theta}{\infty}+\frac{1-\theta}{q+1},

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r}=\frac{\theta}{2}+\frac{1-\theta}{q+1}.

Now, if
\frac{1}{r}\geq\frac{1}{m}+\frac{s}{2}
is satisfied for
r\in(2,q+1)
,
m\in(q+1,\infty)
,
s\in(0,1)
,
then the set of inequalities are satisfied and
we obtain (
B.1
).

∎

Next, we present some results about the multiplication of functions, respective, the multiplication of a function with a distribution.
The notations
F^{s}_{p,q}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and
B^{s}_{p,q}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
,
s\in\mathbb{R},

0<q\leq\infty
,
0<p<\infty
, stand respectively for the Triebel-Lizorkin spaces and Besov spaces.
For the definition of these spaces, we refer to
[
30
, P. 8]
. They translate to classical function spaces
as in e.g.
[
30
, P. 14]
, in particular,

\begin{split}&F_{p,2}^{0}(\mathbb{R}^{d})=L_{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d}),\ 1<p<\infty\ \text{ (Lebesgue spaces)},\\
&F_{p,2}^{m}(\mathbb{R}^{d})=W^{m}_{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d}),\ m\in\mathbb{N},\ <p<\infty\ \text{(Sobolev spaces)},\\
&F_{p,2}^{s}(\mathbb{R}^{d})=H^{s}_{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d}),\ s\in\mathbb{R},\ 1<p<\infty\ \text{(fractional Sobolev spaces)}.\end{split}

(B.2)

Assume that
0<q_{1},q_{2}\leq\infty
and
0<p<\infty
as well as
s_{1}
and
s_{2}
are real numbers such that
s_{1}\leq s_{2}
.

Theorem B.2
.

Assume
s_{1}+s_{2}>d\cdot\max(0,\frac{1}{p}-1)
, and
q\geq\max(q_{1},q_{2})
. Then

•

if
s_{2}>s_{1}
, then
F_{p,q_{1}}^{s_{1}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})\cdot B_{\infty,q_{2}}^{s_{2}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})\hookrightarrow F_{p,q_{1}}^{s_{1}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})
;

•

and, if
s_{1}=s_{2}
then
F_{p,q_{1}}^{s_{1}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})\cdot B_{\infty,q_{2}}^{s_{1}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})\hookrightarrow F_{p,q}^{s_{1}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})
.

Proof.

See
[
30
, p. 229]
.∎

Theorem B.3
.

Let
s_{1}+s_{2}>d\cdot\max(0,\frac{1}{p}-1)
.

•

Assume
s_{2}>\frac{d}{p}
and
q\geq\max(q_{1},q_{2})
. Then, if
s_{2}>s_{1}
,

F_{p,q_{1}}^{s_{1}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})\cdot F_{p,q_{2}}^{s_{2}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})\hookrightarrow F_{p,q_{1}}^{s_{1}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})
;
if
s_{2}=s_{1}
,

F_{p,q_{1}}^{s_{1}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})\cdot F_{p,q_{2}}^{s_{2}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})\hookrightarrow F_{p,q}^{s_{1}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})
;

•

Let
s_{1}=s_{2}=\frac{d}{p}
and
q\geq\max(q_{1},q_{2})
. If
0<p\leq 1
, then
F_{p,q_{1}}^{s_{1}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})\cdot F_{p,q_{2}}^{s_{2}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})\hookrightarrow F_{p,q}^{s_{1}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})
;

•

If
s_{2}<\frac{d}{p}
, then

F_{p,q_{1}}^{s_{1}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})\cdot F_{p,q_{2}}^{s_{2}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})\hookrightarrow F_{p,q}^{s_{1}+s_{2}-\frac{d}{p}}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})
.

Proof.

See
[
30
, p. 190]
.∎

To work on bounded domains let us introduce the extensions. For a bounded domain
{{\mathcal{O}}}\subset{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
for
g\in\mathcal{S}^{\prime}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})
, the restriction of
g
to
{{\mathcal{O}}}
is an element of
\mathcal{D}({{\mathcal{O}}})
and will be denoted by
g\Big|_{{{\mathcal{O}}}}
. For a given function
f:{{\mathcal{O}}}\to{\mathbb{R}}
, an element
g\in\mathcal{S}^{\prime}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})
is called an extension of
f
if
f=g\Big|_{{{\mathcal{O}}}}
. We recall from
[
32
, Definition 5.3, P. 44]
the following definition.

Definition B.4
.

Let
s\in{\mathbb{R}}
and
0<q\leq\infty
.

(i)

if
0<p<\infty
, then we put

F_{p,q}^{s}({{\mathcal{O}}})=\left\{f\in\mathcal{D}^{\prime}({{\mathcal{O}}}):\exists\,g\in F^{s}_{p,q}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})\,\mbox{ with }g\big|_{{{\mathcal{O}}}}=f\right\}

and

|f|_{F_{p,q}^{s}}:=|f|_{F_{p,q}^{s}({{\mathcal{O}}})}=\inf|g|_{F_{p,q}^{s}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})},

where the infimum is taken over all
g\in B_{p,q}^{s}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})
such that
g\big|_{{{\mathcal{O}}}}=f
.

(ii)

if
0<p\leq\infty
, then we put

B_{p,q}^{s}({{\mathcal{O}}})=\left\{f\in\mathcal{D}^{\prime}({{\mathcal{O}}}):\exists\,g\in B^{s}_{p,q}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})\,\mbox{ with }g\big|_{{{\mathcal{O}}}}=f\right\}

and

|f|_{B_{p,q}^{s}}:=|f|_{F_{p,q}^{s}({{\mathcal{O}}})}=\inf|g|_{B_{p,q}^{s}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})},

where the infimum is taken over all
g\in B_{p,q}^{s}({\mathbb{R}}^{d})
such that
g\big|_{{{\mathcal{O}}}}=f
.

In the case where
{{\mathcal{O}}}
is a
C^{\infty}
bounded domain, it is proved in
[
32
, P. 52]
that the relations given in (
B.2
) are still valid. In addition, it is showed in
[
6
, Theorem C.1]
that Theorem
B.2
and Theorem
B.3
are still valid for a
C^{\infty}
bounded domain
{{\mathcal{O}}}\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
in place of
\mathbb{R}^{d}
.
